tbmm-2014-07-19sit03spe0130par0040	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on behalf of the People's Democratic Party on the 19th Amendment of the bill. I salute the General Assembly. With this clause of the bill, amendments are being made to Article 30 of Law 6331, and arrangements are made to assign training programmes, the duration of education and the qualifications of educators, to assume occupational health and safety services at workplaces with fewer than 10 employees and less dangerous classrooms. We believe that the Ministry of Labor and Social Security should apply the opinions of relevant union and vocational rooms and act in this direction and that this clause should be changed accordingly in order to determine matters related to occupational health and safety services and education. Dear lawmakers are creating predictable and preventable accidents in the majority of business accidents. Even with all security measures being taken, accidents can happen, which are literally the same thing as an accident. However, there are problems with calling predictable and preventable accidents "a business accident." Because the expression "accident" gives the impression that there's no one responsible. However, all predictable and preventable accidents have a responsibility. When employers and public authorities take the necessary precautions, workers who do not comply with security instructions are also responsible. However, as the overall situation in our country -- once again in Soma -- employers have not done enough to take the necessary precautions, and the relevant ministries have not taken deterrent measures on business and labour safety. Dear lawmakers, why is it appropriate to say that there are fatal accidents in which employers do not take safety precautions and the ministry does not take deterrent measures? Because it reminds us that if you do nothing to prevent a job murder, a predictable and preventable accident, you're responsible for death. Given that in our country, sanctions regarding business accidents are weak, legal processes are long and often inconclusive, it is best to say that fatal work accidents take place in cases of severe neglect are a crime. However, it's not enough for us to just say that, but the law and the courts to treat such business accidents as work killings. For example, in the United States of America, it is not in vain for courts to prosecute employers for murder in business accidents, and punish employers for murder in cases of fatal business accidents. Mr. President, high-value lawmakers are an important step in encouraging employers to take precautions to prevent business accidents, to increase sentences and immediately conclude cases related to business accidents. Co-operation with workers is the second key step in preventing thousands of workers from losing their lives and losing their lives every year. Third: Taxes can be used to punish companies with a bad report card for job safety and business accidents and to reward companies with good ration cards. Taxes can be imposed on companies whose report card is bad, while companies whose report card is good can be used for tax cuts. In addition, incentives for increased job security can be given in the form of insurance incentives. The insurance premiums can be lowered for companies and employees with a good job security report card. Companies investing in business safety and occupational health can be given various economic incentives. For example, a certain percentage of investment in business security can be covered by the public. Finally: non-fiscal incentives can also be given to increase job security. Companies that are successful in boosting business security and reducing business accidents can be presented and presented with symbolic awards, while companies that fail to prevent business accidents can be announced and exposed to the public. Once again, Turkey's underprivileged conditions on business and labour health and safety cannot be met by superficial approaches to pre-election laws, I salute the General Assembly.
